# Part 03 — Code Anchor Atlas (GENERATED — do not hand-edit)

Generated by `scripts/accounting-bible/build_atlas.py` at commit `aa1f7a8adcc926eb20b4e5f60e335bc6bf694175`.

**How to use:** search for a symbol, read its `file:line` PIN, then open the file *at that line*.
Before you edit anything, run `git diff aa1f7a8a -- <file>`. If the file moved, regenerate this part.

Columns: `prod importers` = non-test files that import the module. **0 prod importers = dead code under rule 50**
(unless it is a pure `*-core.ts` consumed only by a `*-service.ts` you plan to build, or a page/route).
Pages, routes and server actions (`src/app/**`) are entry points; they have no importers by design.

## B. Not imported by production code (rule 50 watch list)

A pure core consumed only by tests is *not yet wired*. Every row here is either (a) a planned
feeder awaiting its service (see Part 09 slice plan), (b) a library of constants/authorities
read only by tests (acceptable if it says so), or (c) genuinely dead. Part 04 classifies them.
